### [191] [Refusal geometry reflects refusal training: diverse refusal prefixes can raise stable rank and weaken refusal vector ablation attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25390)
*Andrey Labunets*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究以OLMo-2-0425-1B-Instruct为案例，揭示了语言模型中拒绝行为（refusal）为何集中在低维方向/子空间：拒绝补全首个token的损失导致的激活更新塑造了拒绝方向和子空间。研究发现拒绝方向的可移除性与重复的拒绝开头（repetitive refusal starts）相关，这种重复源于梯度和拒绝特征在低维子空间中的集中；通过多样化的拒绝开头进行微调，可提高梯度和激活变化的稳定秩，使拒绝更难被向量消融攻击移除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的对抗性越狱（jailbreak）研究已发现，对齐模型的拒绝行为可由单一激活方向或共享的低维拒绝子空间介导，消融这些方向可抑制拒绝而几乎保留其他能力。但尚不清楚为何安全关键特征会涌现并形成集中的低维结构，本文旨在从训练动态角度解释其成因，并探索如何加固这一机制（hardening）。

Method: 以OLMo-2-0425-1B-Instruct为案例：1) 分析拒绝训练过程中由拒绝补全首个token损失产生的激活更新，解释其如何塑造拒绝方向与拒绝子空间；2) 跨数据集追踪拒绝方向在训练动态中的形成与演化；3) 结合冻结模型分析与受控合成微调实验，将重复拒绝开头与梯度/特征的集中低维结构及可移除性（brittleness）联系起来；4) 测试多样化拒绝开头是否提升梯度和激活变化的稳定秩。

Result: 研究发现拒绝几何结构直接反映了拒绝训练过程：首个token损失驱动的更新生成拒绝方向。拒绝方向的脆弱性源于重复的拒绝开头，后者对应梯度和拒绝特征在低维子空间的集中。受控合成微调显示，多样化的拒绝开头可提高梯度和激活变化的稳定秩，使向量消融攻击更难移除拒绝行为，即存在“加固杠杆”。

Conclusion: 拒绝行为的低维集中结构并非偶然，而是拒绝训练动态的产物；其脆弱性可被理解并缓解。通过增加拒绝开头的多样性可提升拒绝特征的鲁棒性，为设计更抗消融攻击的对齐模型提供可操作的训练原则。

Abstract: Refusal training protects AI models from jailbreaks by training models to decline unsafe queries, reducing the risk of misuse. Recent work finds that refusal behavior in aligned language models can be mediated by a single activation direction or a low-dimensional refusal subspace shared across harmful prompts: ablating those directions suppresses refusals while largely preserves other model capabilities. Yet it remains unclear why safety-critical features in a wide range of models emerge and concentrated, low-dimensional structure. In a case study of OLMo-2-0425-1B-Instruct we find that the refusal geometry reflects refusal training: activation updates resulting from refusal-completion first-token losses explain the resulting refusal direction and refusal subspace. We study refusal directions through the training dynamics across refusal datasets and reveal that their brittleness is associated with repetitive refusal starts, which in turn is linked to concentration of gradients and refusal features in a low-dimensional subspace. Across frozen-model analyses and controlled synthetic fine-tuning, we find evidence of a hardening lever: diverse refusal starts can raise stable ranks of gradients and activation changes, making refusals harder to remove with a vector ablation attack.

</details>


### [192] [Joint Initialization of Flux Networks and Effective Multiplication Factor for Physics-Informed Neural Networks Solving Neutron Diffusion Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25443)
*Qin Hang,Yangdi Yi,Jiayi Li,Xu Wang,Heng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出联合初始化物理信息神经网络（JI-PINN），通过利用K特征值问题的低分辨率近似解来联合初始化通量网络参数与keff，并在物理约束下联合优化，从而在保持精度的同时显著提升keff计算效率，并在多个基准算例中验证了计算时间减少25.4%–49.4%，且减少了异常结果的出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反应堆堆芯中子学分析中，有效增殖因子（keff）的高效计算是一项重要任务。基于PINN的方法将中子扩散方程与边界条件融入网络训练以求解中子通量分布和keff，但现有PINN方法的keff计算效率仍有提升空间。

Method: 提出JI-PINN方法：先用K特征值问题的低分辨率近似解构造通量网络参数与keff的联合初始状态，随后两者在物理约束下进行联合优化。通过低分辨率解提供良好初值，避免随机初始化带来的收敛慢与异常结果问题。

Result: 在二维两群两材料算例、IAEA 2D基准题、二维两群四材料算例和三维单群算例上验证，总计算时间分别减少25.4%、38.2%、49.4%和28.9%，同时保持相当的解精度；keff显著偏离参考值的异常结果出现频率也降低。

Conclusion: JI-PINN为利用PINN求解中子扩散K特征值问题提供了一种更高效、更稳健的初始化策略。

Abstract: Efficient determination of the effective multiplication factor (keff) is an important computational task in reactor core neutronics analysis. Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) incorporate neutron diffusion equations and boundary conditions into network training to efficiently determine the neutron flux distribution and keff. To further improve the efficiency of keff calculations using PINNs, a Joint Initialization Physics-Informed Neural Network (JI-PINN) is proposed in this work. In this method, a low-resolution approximate solution to the K-eigenvalue problem is used to construct a joint initial state for the flux network parameters and keff, and both are then jointly optimized under physical constraints. The proposed method was validated on a two-dimensional two-group two-material case, the IAEA 2D benchmark, a two-dimensional two-group four-material case, and a three-dimensional single-group case. For these test cases, the total computational time was reduced by 25.4%, 38.2%, 49.4%, and 28.9%, respectively, while comparable solution accuracy was maintained. The occurrence of anomalous results associated with marked deviations of keff from the reference value was also reduced. The proposed method provides a more efficient and robust initialization strategy for solving neutron diffusion K-eigenvalue problem with PINNs.

</details>


### [193] [Resolving Multi-Modal Regression by Difference-Quotient-Based Clustering:Fast Coarse Conditional-Label Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25467)
*Huang Weiquan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多模态回归中的“均值坍缩”病态（在平方损失下，非受限回归器收敛到条件均值，该均值远离所有 K>1 个模态），将其归因于“逐对矛盾”（输入近乎相同而输出相距甚远），并提出差分商聚类（DQC）：按“簇内输出-输入差异”最小化对数据分区，将每个样本分配到使其最大矛盾比最小的簇，再用所得标签训练 logits 生成器与条件网络。由于测试时生成模态未知，采用对所有 K 个真实输出的最小平方误差（minMSE）评估。在合成基准（K=5,10）上，DQC 的测试 minMSE 为 0.19（K=5, nx=500），而 oracle 为 0.09、随机标签为 1.08、均值坍缩为 1.33。经验规律包括：更大簇内矛盾需要更深网络，oracle 标签比簇导出标签用更少样本即可泛化。聚类是可并行化的硬 O(n²/2) 前端，用于粗略条件分配，减轻下游生成精化的负担；未来工作提出基于残差的第二阶段再聚类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态回归在平方损失下存在均值坍缩病理：模型退化为预测条件均值，该均值落在所有模态之外，无法表征多值映射。作者将这一失败归因于训练数据中的逐对矛盾（几乎相同的输入对应差异极大的输出），并以解决该“矛盾”作为设计聚类准则的出发点。

Method: 提出差分商聚类（DQC）：以最小化簇内输出-输入之间的差异（discrepancy）为目标对数据分区；每个样本被分配到使其最大矛盾比最小的簇；随后基于聚类产生的标签训练 logits 生成器与条件网络。聚类为硬划分、可并行化的 O(n²/2) 前端，仅做粗略条件分配，可由下游生成网络精化。

Result: 在合成基准（K=5、10）上，DQC 的测试 minMSE 为 0.19（K=5，nx=500），显著优于随机标签（1.08）与均值坍缩（1.33），接近 oracle 标签（0.09）。另观察到两条经验规律：簇内矛盾越大所需网络越深；oracle 标签比聚类导出标签以更少样本即可实现泛化。

Conclusion: DQC 通过显式消解逐对输入矛盾来完成粗略的模态分配，有效缓解均值坍缩，且作为高效前端降低了生成精化的负担；同时指出两阶段策略（在残差误差上再聚类）作为未来工作，暗示存在进一步逼近 oracle 性能的空间。

Abstract: Multimodal regression suffers from the mean-collapse pathology: under squared loss, an unconstrained regressor converges to the conditional mean, which for K > 1 lies away from all modes. We attribute this failure to pairwise contradictions--samples with nearly identical inputs but distant outputs--and propose Difference-Quotient Clustering (DQC), which partitions data to minimize intra-cluster output-vs-input discrepancy. Each sample is assigned to the cluster that minimizes its maximum contradiction ratio; a logits generator and a conditional network are then trained on the resulting labels. Since the generating modality is unknown at test time, we evaluate via minimum squared error (minMSE) against all K true outputs. On synthetic benchmarks (K=5, 10), DQC achieves test minMSE 0.19 (K=5, nx=500), versus 0.09 for an oracle, 1.08 for random labels, and 1.33 for mean collapse. We observe two empirical regularities: larger intra-cluster contradictions require deeper networks, and oracle labels generalize from fewer samples than cluster-derived equivalents. The clustering is a hard, parallelizable O(n^2/2) front-end for coarse conditional assignment, reducing the burden of downstream generative refinement. A second-stage re-clustering on residual errors is outlined as future work.

</details>


### [194] [A Storage-Retrieval Gap in Parametric Knowledge Graph Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25489)
*Martino M. L. Pulici,Cuong Xuan Chu,Evgeny Kharlamov,Volker Tresp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究将知识图谱离线编译为按实体划分的LoRA适配器库，作为以权重注入方式查询的参数化知识层，替代查询时向上下文注入子图的图谱检索增强生成（Graph RAG）。实验表明适配器能编码并可泛化地存储事实验证知识（单值关系上EM增益+0.243），但基于嵌入或权值空间几何的相似性检索均无法恢复该知识（随机水平），揭示"存储可行但不可检索"的核心矛盾，并提出学习式查询条件化组合机制为开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图谱检索增强生成（Graph RAG）在每次查询时把检索到的子图放入上下文窗口，导致持续的token成本并每次调用都暴露源数据。作者探索替代方案：离线将知识图谱编译成每个实体一个LoRA适配器的参数库，作为参数化知识层，通过注入权重而非文本进行查询，实现零查询时上下文成本。

Method: 在MetaQA数据集上，将知识图谱离线编译为按实体划分的LoRA适配器库；子图训练适配器以编码上下文无关的事实知识；查询时通过权重注入而非文本检索。实验设置了闭卷基座模型、oracle（正确适配器）选择、嵌入相似性检索和权值空间几何检索等多组对比，并量化字节与上下文字符token成本。

Result: （1）子图训练的适配器可编码事实知识并泛化到未见问题：单值关系上EM增益+0.243，基座模型近乎闭卷盲（0.007），仅正确的适配器能恢复该知识（oracle相比基座gap +0.283）。（2）知识无法通过相似性恢复：嵌入与权值空间几何检索均为随机水平，因为语义相邻实体的适配器不含答案——知识局部存储、不迁移。（3）权值几何与子图语义相关（ρ=+0.329），但与功能可检索性无关。（4）量化了相对Graph RAG的字节与上下文token成本并讨论部署含义。

Conclusion: 参数化知识图谱记忆在知识存储上可行，但中心开放问题在于如何通过语义相似性之外的机制选择和组合正确的适配器——这直接催生学习式、查询条件化的组合机制作为后续研究方向。

Abstract: Graph retrieval-augmented generation places retrieved subgraphs into the model's context window at query time, paying a recurring token cost and exposing source data on every call. We study an alternative: compiling a knowledge graph offline into a bank of LoRA adapters, one per entity, that serve as a parametric knowledge layer queried by injecting weights rather than text, at zero query-time context cost. On the MetaQA dataset, we find that subgraph-trained adapters encode context-free factual knowledge that generalizes to unseen questions: on single-valued relations the adapter gains $+0.243$ exact-match score over a base model that is nearly blind closed-book ($0.007$), and only the correct adapter recovers this knowledge (an oracle gap of $+0.283$ over the base model). However, the stored knowledge is not recoverable by similarity: given a query with no subgraph, embedding-based and weight-space geometry retrieval both perform at chance, because a semantically neighbouring entity's adapter does not contain the answer - knowledge is stored locally and does not transfer. Weight geometry correlates with subgraph semantics ($ρ= +0.329$) but not with functional retrievability. We quantify the byte and context-token costs against graph retrieval-augmented generation and discuss deployment implications. Our results establish that parametric knowledge graph memory is feasible for storing knowledge, and identify selecting and composing the right adapters by a mechanism other than semantic similarity as the central open problem - motivating a learned, query-conditioned composition mechanism.

</details>


### [195] [FedQoS: Federated QoS-Risk Learning for Heterogeneous Indoor-Outdoor Access Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25496)
*Nguyen Van Thieu,Ti Ti Nguyen,Ons Aouedi,Zerihun Huruy,Vu Nguyen Ha,Symeon Chatzinotas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedQoS 是一个联邦式 QoS 风险学习框架，用于预测候选接入链路的未来可靠性，并辅助接入节点选择，无需集中用户级网络数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在动态异构的室内外环境中，仅依赖瞬时无线电测量无法捕捉由移动性、阻塞、业务负载和资源竞争导致的未来 QoS 劣化，可靠性接入选择具有挑战性；同时用户级网络数据不宜集中化。

Method: 每个接入节点基于本地观测的网络日志（含无线电、业务、负载和服务上下文特征）进行本地学习；通过联邦聚合训练全局 QoS 风险预测器；模型估算每个候选链路的 QoS 失效概率，控制器据此选择可靠接入节点。

Result: 基于 Sionna 框架构建物理合成室内外无线数据集，覆盖正常流量、移动性、事件驱动拥塞和非 IID 客户端观测。仿真显示基于学习的接入选择相比基于信号和历史 QoS 的启发式方法显著降低 QoS 失效率；FedQoS 达到接近集中式预测的性能，在轻度非 IID 下提供明显可靠性增益，在严重非 IID 下仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: 联邦 QoS 风险学习在动态无线环境中可实现可靠、数据本地的接入选择，具有较大应用潜力。

Abstract: Reliable access selection in dynamic and heterogeneous indoor-outdoor environments is challenging because instantaneous radio measurements alone cannot capture future QoS degradation caused by mobility, blockage, traffic load, and resource competition. This paper proposes FedQoS, a federated QoS-risk learning framework for predicting the future reliability of candidate access links and supporting access-node selection without centralizing user-level network data. In FedQoS, each access node locally learns from its observed network logs, including radio, traffic, load, and service-context features, while a global QoS-risk predictor is trained through federated aggregation. The learned model estimates the probability of QoS failure for each candidate link, and the controller uses these risk scores to select reliable access nodes under dynamic network conditions. To evaluate the framework, we construct physics-based synthetic indoor-outdoor wireless datasets using the Sionna framework, covering normal traffic, mobility, event-driven congestion, and non-IID client observations. Simulation results show that learning-based access selection substantially reduces the QoS-failure rate compared with signal-based and historical-QoS heuristic methods. FedQoS achieves near-centralized predictive performance and provides clear reliability gains under mild non-IID data while remaining competitive under the more challenging severe non-IID condition. These results demonstrate the potential of federated QoS-risk learning for reliable, data-local access selection in dynamic wireless environments.

</details>


### [196] [Resilient Decentralized Wireless Federated Learning via Gradient Tracking with AdamW](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25535)
*Nguyen Van Thieu,Ti Ti Nguyen,Ons Aouedi,Vu Nguyen Ha,Symeon Chatzinotas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 QEF-GT-AdamW，一种面向无线IoT边缘网络的通信高效且抗中断的分散式学习算法。该方法融合梯度跟踪（缓解非IID数据影响）、AdamW自适应优化（提升训练稳定性）以及带误差反馈的双流有偏量化（降低模型与追踪信息交换的通信负载），并针对不可靠广播通信采用本地回退策略。作者显式建模带宽、发射功率、空中时间约束与衰落信道对分散学习性能的影响，并在压缩与不可靠无线通信条件下给出收敛性保证。在异构MNIST与CIFAR-10上的实验表明，该算法在有限无线资源下相较代表性基线在鲁棒性、收敛速度及精度-通信权衡上均有优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线IoT边缘网络中的分散式学习（DecL）需要在异构本地数据和通信受限的无线链路下可靠运行，但现有分散式优化方案在严格空中时间预算、衰落信道和丢包约束下会带来大量通信开销及性能退化，亟需一种通信高效且能应对传输中断的算法。

Method: QEF-GT-AdamW 采用三种核心技术：1）梯度跟踪（gradient tracking）缓解非独立同分布（non-IID）数据的影响；2）AdamW自适应优化提升训练稳定性；3）双流有偏量化与误差反馈机制降低模型和追踪信息交换的通信负载。此外，针对不可靠广播通信，采用本地回退策略处理调度失败的数据包；文中还显式建立了带宽、发射功率、空中时间约束及衰落信道对DecL性能的模型，并给出压缩与不可靠无线通信下的收敛性保证。

Result: 在异构MNIST与CIFAR-10任务上的实验表明，QEF-GT-AdamW相较于代表性DecL基线，在鲁棒性和收敛性能上持续提升，并在受限无线资源下实现更优的精度-通信权衡。

Conclusion: QEF-GT-AdamW 提供了一种兼顾通信效率与中断鲁棒性的分散式无线学习方案，能够在不IID数据、压缩通信及包丢失等实际无线约束下保持稳定收敛，为资源受限的无线IoT边缘网络提供可信赖的学习算法。

Abstract: Wireless Internet-of-Things (IoT) edge networks require decentralized learning (DecL) methods that can operate reliably under both heterogeneous local data and communication-constrained wireless links. However, existing decentralized optimization schemes often incur substantial communication overhead and degraded performance when transmissions are constrained by strict airtime budgets, fading channels, and packet losses. This paper proposes QEF-GT-AdamW, a communication-efficient and outage-resilient algorithm for DecL over wireless communication (WCom) networks. The proposed method combines gradient tracking to mitigate the effect of non-IID data, AdamW-based adaptive optimization to improve training stability, and dual-stream biased quantization with error feedback to reduce communication payloads for both model and tracking exchanges. To address unreliable broadcast communication, the proposed framework further employs a local fallback strategy when scheduled packets are not successfully received. We explicitly model the effect of bandwidth, transmit power, airtime constraints, and fading channels on DecL performance, and establish convergence guarantees for the proposed algorithm under compressed and unreliable wireless communication. Experimental results on heterogeneous MNIST and CIFAR-10 settings show that QEF-GT-AdamW consistently improves robustness and convergence performance over representative DecL baselines while achieving favorable accuracy-communication trade-offs under limited wireless resources.

</details>


### [197] [Reflection Steering: Disentangling Reflection from Reasoning in Activation Space for Token-Efficient Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25542)
*Jiarui Hu,Zhiyuan Wen,Xiaoyun Liu,Jiaxing Shen,Yu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Reflection Steering，一种无需训练的控制大语言模型反射（反思）计算量的框架。通过逐层对比反思与非反思隐状态、用 PCA 去噪、并与通用推理方向正交化，得到纯净的反思方向；再基于小样本跨强度校准筛选稳定层，对残差流施加有界投影移除。在两个基准、三个开源模型上相比 SOTA 激活引导方法，平均减少推理 token 16.9%，并引入有界的干预强度参数 α 以在生成时权衡 token 节省、准确率与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型常产生包含验证、修订和回溯的推理轨迹，但当反思只是重复检查已确立的结果时，会浪费推理 token 并增加延迟。现有反思引导方法在预设层上叠加由标签推导的均值差方向，但其与推理和长度信号纠缠在一起，使准确率-效率权衡不稳定。

Method: 1) 在每一层对比反思型与非反思型隐状态，得到反射方向；2) 用 PCA 去噪；3) 将反射方向与通用推理方向正交化，以解耦与推理/长度信号的纠缠；4) 在小型数据集上对每层跨多个干预强度校准，仅保留稳定层；5) 对残差流激活施加有界投影移除，限制早期层干预的下游放大；6) 引入有界反射干预强度参数 α，支持部署时调节。

Result: 在两个公开基准和三个开源权重 LLM 上，对比 SOTA 激活引导基线，在六组匹配设置中平均减少推理 token 16.9%，且能通过 α 平衡 token 节省、准确率与生成稳定性。

Conclusion: Reflection Steering 通过正交解耦反射相关激活与通用推理、去噪（PCA）及稳定层筛选，实现了更稳健的准确率-效率权衡；有界 α 参数使其可在部署阶段灵活调整，是一种训练免费的反思计算量控制方案。

Abstract: Large reasoning models often produce reasoning traces with verification, revision, and backtracking. When reflection merely re-checks established results, it wastes reasoning tokens and increases latency. Most existing reflection steering methods add a label-derived mean-difference direction across preset layers, but its entanglement with reasoning and length signals destabilizes the accuracy-efficiency trade-off. In this paper, we propose Reflection Steering, a training-free framework for controlling reflection-associated computation within LLMs by disentangling reflection-related activations from general reasoning. Specifically, we contrast reflective and non-reflective hidden states at each LLM layer, denoise the resulting reflection directions with PCA, and orthogonalize them against general-reasoning directions. To limit downstream amplification from early-layer interventions, we calibrate each layer across multiple intervention strengths on a small set, retain only stable layers, and apply bounded projection removal to their residual-stream activations. We conduct extensive experiments across two public benchmarks and three open-weight LLMs against state-of-the-art activation-steering baselines. Results show that Reflection Steering reduces reasoning tokens by 16.9% on average across six matched settings. Besides, our method further introduces a bounded reflection intervention-strength parameter $α$, enabling deployment-time adjustment to balance token savings, accuracy, and generation stability.

</details>


### [198] [Interpreting Protein Language Model Embeddings via Orthogonal Projection for Protein Fitness Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25548)
*Paulo Yanez Sarmiento,Pia Francesca Rissom,Manuel Pfeuffer,Marco Simnacher,Jordan F. Safer,Sumaiya Iqbal,Henrike O. Heyne,Nadja Klein,Bernhard Y. Renard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用正交投影技术（扩展至高阶与交互效应）从蛋白质语言模型（PLM）嵌入中去除可解释的生化特征，通过消融实验和方差分析证明PLM嵌入编码了与生化性质相关的模式，并量化了这些特征对蛋白质适应度预测的贡献。该方法计算高效且可迁移至其他任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质语言模型（PLM）的嵌入在蛋白质适应度预测等下游任务中表现优异，但其嵌入缺乏直接可解释性，无法明确了解嵌入中编码了哪些生物化学特征，这阻碍了对模型预测机制的理解。

Method: 采用正交投影技术，将已知表格特征的线性效应从PLM嵌入中去除，并将其扩展到高阶效应和交互效应；通过消除可解释生化特征的影响来构建消融嵌入，并训练下游分类器预测蛋白质适应度。

Result: 消融研究表明，去除生化特征后，仅依赖嵌入训练的下游分类器在蛋白质适应度预测上的性能下降；进一步评估显示，这些生化特征解释了分类器预测方差的很大一部分，从而量化了它们对预测的贡献。

Conclusion: PLM嵌入确实编码了与生化性质相关的模式，且可以定量评估这些模式对蛋白质适应度预测的贡献；该方法计算高效，不限于当前的特征和嵌入，可便捷地推广到蛋白质适应度预测之外的多种问题场景。

Abstract: Recently, there has been a growing adoption of protein language models (PLMs) in biomedical science. Their embeddings provide a rich numerical representation of protein sequences which achieve state-of-the-art performance on several downstream tasks including protein fitness prediction. However, PLM embeddings are not directly interpretable and, thereby, it remains unclear what features they encode. To gain insight into which biochemical properties of the protein are driving the prediction, we leverage an orthogonal projection technique that removes linear effects of known tabular features from embeddings and extend it to high-order and interaction effects. In this way, we remove the effects of interpretable biochemical features from PLM embeddings. In an ablation study, we show that this leads to a decrease in performance for a downstream classifier trained only on the embeddings to predict protein fitness. In an additional evaluation, we find that these biochemical features explain a substantial part of the variance in the predictions of this classifier. Hence, we can show that PLM embeddings encode patterns correlated with biochemical properties and quantify their contribution to predicting protein fitness. This computationally efficient approach is not limited to the features or embeddings considered here and is readily transferable to problem settings beyond protein fitness prediction.

</details>


### [199] [Beyond Optimal Rates in Stochastic Optimization: Trajectory-Adaptive Stopping Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25551)
*Liviu Aolaritei,Lucas Lévy,Francis Bach,Michael I. Jordan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对强凸随机优化中的SGD，构建了轨迹自适应的、逐点有效的上置信序列（对末次迭代到最优点的平方距离以及加权平均的次优性），使得SGD可以在任意数据依赖的停止时间停止，同时保持统计有效性。这些界在全时间上同时成立，最坏情况下达到最优的1/t衰减率（至多差迭代对数因子），并自适应于实际实现的随机梯度。作者开发了新的递归置信序列技术和一个通用的时间一致经验Bernstein不等式（适用于条件均值时变、可预测范围可无界增长的适应过程），并将其扩展到小批量SGD，利用批内二阶矩结构。数值实验表明，所得停止规则比自然确定性水平迭代次数少几个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SGD分析通常在算法运行前确定的固定时间水平给出保证，而实际中停止决策是自适应地基于轨迹观测做出的。这一不匹配导致根本性的认证问题：固定时间保证在数据依赖的停止时间处通常不再有效；而基于最坏情况界推导的确定性水平又过于保守。因此需要能在任意停止时间都成立的轨迹自适应置信界，以实现"一旦达到预定精度即可停止"而不牺牲统计有效性。

Method: 作者将演化的SGD轨迹视为一个序列实验，其观测提供关于未知优化误差的证据。具体方法包括：(1) 构建完全可观测、轨迹自适应的上置信序列，同时覆盖末次迭代的平方距离和加权平均的次优性；(2) 开发新的递归置信序列技术；(3) 建立适用于适应过程（具有时变条件均值和可能无界增长的可预测范围）的通用时间一致经验Bernstein不等式；(4) 将置信序列构造扩展到小批量SGD，利用批内实现的二阶矩结构。

Result: 所得置信序列同时在全时间上成立，最坏情况下达到最优的1/t收敛速率（至多差迭代对数因子），并能自适应实际实现的随机梯度。数值实验证明，由此得到的自适应停止规则相比自然的确定性时间水平，可减少几个数量级的迭代次数，证明其显著降低了保守性。

Conclusion: 该工作从统计推断（时序实验）视角重新审视SGD的停止问题，提供了首个能够支持数据依赖停止时间的严格有效置信序列框架，将优化保证从"固定水平"推广到"随时可停"，既保持了最优收敛速率又大幅节省计算成本，并为小批量SGD给出了利用二阶矩结构的进一步改进。

Abstract: Stochastic gradient descent (SGD) is typically analyzed at a deterministic horizon chosen before the algorithm is run, even though practical stopping decisions are made adaptively by inspecting the evolving trajectory. This mismatch creates a fundamental certification problem: fixed-time guarantees do not generally remain valid at data-dependent stopping times, while deterministic horizons derived from worst-case bounds can be highly conservative. We address this problem for strongly convex stochastic optimization by constructing fully observable, trajectory-adaptive upper confidence sequences for the squared distance of the last iterate to the optimizer and the suboptimality of a weighted average. These bounds hold simultaneously over time, attain the optimal $1/t$ decay rate up to iterated-logarithmic factors in the worst case, and adapt to the realized stochastic gradients, allowing SGD to stop as soon as a prescribed accuracy is certified without sacrificing statistical validity. Our approach treats the evolving SGD trajectory as a sequential experiment whose observations provide evidence about the unknown optimization error. To formalize this perspective, we develop new recursive confidence-sequence techniques and a general time-uniform empirical Bernstein inequality for adapted processes with time-varying conditional means and predictable ranges that may grow without bound. We further extend these confidence-sequence constructions to minibatch SGD, with the empirical Bernstein bounds exploiting the realized second-moment structure within each minibatch. Numerical experiments show that the resulting stopping rules can require several orders of magnitude fewer iterations than natural deterministic horizons.

</details>


### [200] [Physics-Informed Foresight Pruning for Sparse PINN Solvers of Nonlinear PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25564)
*Ahmad Ishaque Karimi,Uvini Balasuriya Mudiyanselage,Kookjin Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究稀疏PirateNet PDE求解器在初始化时的前视剪枝问题，提出物理信息谱感知剪枝（PI-SAP），利用PDE残差灵敏度作为显著性指标，与标准NTK-SAP对比。实验表明PI-SAP在Gray-Scott方程残差保真度上更一致、在激进稀疏下具有竞争力，但没有任何单一准则在所有方程和稀疏度下普遍最优；同时PINN-NTK诊断显示残差保真度、解精度与核条件数是不同目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN常依赖过参数化模型来同时优化耦合的解目标与微分残差目标，导致其所需容量不明确、剪枝应保留哪些参数也不清楚；标准NTK-SAP旨在保留输出侧训练动力学，但可能忽略主要通过控制方程导数产生影响的参数。因此需要针对PDE残差敏感性的剪枝准则。

Method: 提出PI-SAP（物理信息谱感知剪枝），基于PDE残差的灵敏度分配参数显著性；采用前视剪枝（foresight pruning）在初始化阶段对稀疏PirateNet进行剪枝；并通过小批量PINN-NTK诊断评估残差保真度、解精度与核条件数等目标。

Result: 在Gray-Scott方程、复金兹堡-朗道方程、Burgers方程和线性对流方程上的实验显示：PI-SAP更一致地保持Gray-Scott残差保真度，且在激进稀疏水平下与NTK-SAP具有竞争力；但没有一种剪枝准则在所有方程或稀疏度下普遍最优。

Conclusion: 剪枝准则应显式平衡解侧与残差侧的训练动力学；残差保真度、解精度和核条件数是相互独立的目标，未来可针对此设计更均衡的剪枝方法。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) often rely on over-parameterized models to optimize coupled solution and differential-residual objectives, leaving unclear how much capacity is necessary and what pruning should preserve. We study foresight pruning at initialization for sparse PirateNet PDE solvers. Standard neural tangent kernel spectrum-aware pruning (NTK-SAP) aims to preserve output-side training dynamics but may overlook parameters whose main influence arises through derivatives in the governing equations. We introduce physics-informed spectrum-aware pruning (PI-SAP), which assigns saliency using sensitivity of the PDE residual. Experiments on the Gray-Scott equations, complex Ginzburg-Landau equation, Burgers' equation, and linear convection equation show that PI-SAP more consistently preserves Gray-Scott residual fidelity and is competitive under aggressive sparsity. However, no criterion is uniformly optimal across equations or sparsity levels. Small-batch PINN-NTK diagnostics further show that residual fidelity, solution accuracy, and kernel conditioning are distinct objectives, motivating pruning methods that explicitly balance solution-side and residual-side training dynamics during optimization.

</details>


### [201] [Beyond Scaling: Self-Evolving LLM Agents for Hardware Kernel Optimization via an Experience-Driven Workflow and Experience Graph Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25570)
*Siyuan Chen,Runlin Hou,Shenxiu Wu,Yansong Sun,Junming Cao,Yiyu Zhang,Shudi Shao,Junhao Qiu,Zhichao Lu,Qingfu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KOPE 是一个面向硬件内核优化的经验驱动框架，通过 Experience Graph Memory 记录优化轨迹（含正确性与性能反馈），并利用 Active Context Management and Injection 在固定 token 预算内检索相关经验，实现跨任务的持续学习，在 GLM-5.2 设置下其算子级加速比几何均值是最强基线 CANNBot 的 1.54 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件内核优化需要反复进行编译、正确性测试、性能剖析与修订。LLM 智能体虽可自动化部分流程，且更强的基座模型、更长上下文与更长执行视野提升了单任务内优化效果，但现有内核优化智能体很少保留决策、其观测到的执行反馈以及后续基于该证据的决策；而完整保留所有历史轨迹也不可行，因为不断膨胀的历史会挤占当前任务的上下文。

Method: KOPE 将正确性与性能反馈记录在 Experience Graph Memory 中，该图结构保留决策顺序、观测结果与备选分支，使目标硬件上收集的证据能指导后续优化步骤和任务；同时采用 Active Context Management and Injection，在固定 token 预算下检索并注入相关经验，避免被动累积历史带来的上下文膨胀。

Result: 在相同 GLM-5.2 设置下，KOPE 每算子加速比的几何均值是 CANNBot 的 1.54 倍。在完整的 53 算子消融实验中，Active Context Management and Injection 将通过率从 60.0% 提升至 84.6%，评估器报告的正向字段几何均值从 0.0382 提升至 0.0661，并将优化 token 消耗从 15.9B 降至 1.113B；启用 Experience Graph Memory 将全套件通过率从 55.2% 提升至 84.6%，在有效计时对比上取得 1.43 倍几何均值加速。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，在基座模型保持不变的情况下，通过外部经验实现持续优化是可行的：经验图记忆加主动上下文注入机制能够在固定 token 预算内显著提升通过率、优化效率与加速比，表明智能体可在不扩大上下文负担的前提下从历史优化轨迹中持续获益。

Abstract: Hardware kernel optimization requires repeated compilation, correctness testing, profiling, and revision. LLM agents can automate parts of this process, and stronger foundation models, longer context windows, and longer execution horizons have improved optimization within individual tasks. These advances alone do not enable an agent to learn from completed optimization runs. Existing kernel-optimization agents seldom preserve a decision, its observed execution feedback, and the later decisions that use that evidence. Retaining every prior trajectory is also impractical because an expanding history competes with the current task for context. We present KOPE, an experience-driven framework for hardware kernel optimization. KOPE records optimization trajectories with correctness and performance feedback in Experience Graph Memory, then uses Active Context Management and Injection to retrieve relevant experience under a fixed token budget. The graph retains decision order, observed outcomes, and alternative branches, allowing evidence collected on the target hardware to inform later optimization steps and tasks. Under the same GLM-5.2 setting, the geometric mean of KOPE's per-operator speedups is $1.54\times$ that of CANNBot, the strongest competing baseline. In a complete 53-operator ablation, Active Context Management and Injection raises pass rate from 60.0\% to 84.6\%, increases the evaluator-reported positive-field geometric mean from 0.0382 to 0.0661, and reduces optimization token consumption from 15.9B to 1.113B tokens relative to passive agent-led context construction. Enabling Experience Graph Memory raises full-suite pass rate from 55.2\% to 84.6\% and yields a $1.43\times$ geometric-mean speedup on valid timing comparisons. These results support continual optimization through external experience while the foundation model remains fixed.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [202] [WaveOp-LiteFM: Lightweight Neural-Operator Flow Matching for Satellite-to-Radar Precipitation Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25818)
*Chunlei Shi,Yecheng Zhang,Yufeng Zhu,Dan Niu,Yichao Dong,Yongchao Feng,Junming Hou*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 提出 WaveOp-LiteFM，一种基于谱-局部-小波（SLW）块的轻量级神经算子流匹配框架，用于卫星到雷达（S2R）降水反演。该框架在像素空间实现高效稳定的流匹配，通过频率解耦与输入自适应门控机制融合特征，配合跳跃门替代代价高昂的通道拼接，在 SEVIR 与中国东南数据集上取得最先进性能并大幅降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式流匹配模型在 S2R 反演中面临两难：像素空间公式受限于基于注意力的 U-Net 速度网络的高昂计算代价，而潜空间建模则损失精细降水细节或难以处理稀疏目标。作者旨在解决这一效率与质量间的关键权衡。

Method: 1) 提出基于 SLW 块的轻量级速度骨干网络，将降水特征解耦为三个频域分支：谱分支捕捉大尺度层状组织，局部分支建模短程交互，小波分支增强尖锐结构并抑制噪声高频响应；2) 引入输入自适应门控机制动态融合三分支特征；3) 设计跳跃门，在解码器中通过加性融合高效整合编码器特征，避免传统 U-Net 的通道拼接开销；4) 在像素空间直接执行流匹配，兼顾效率与细节保真。

Result: 在 SEVIR 与中国东南数据集上取得最先进的检索性能，同时显著降低计算成本；在中国全域大范围推理及近期台风“巴威”案例中，验证了其在真实大规模场景下保持可靠检索质量的能力。

Conclusion: WaveOp-LiteFM 实证表明：通过频率解耦、自适应门控与轻量级重连设计，能够在像素空间实现高效而稳定的流匹配反演，破解了 S2R 检索中计算成本与细粒度质量不可兼得的困境，并具备面向大范围真实应用的泛化能力。

Abstract: Satellite-to-radar (S2R) retrieval refers to estimating ground-based radar precipitation from geostationary satellite observations, enabling precipitation monitoring in regions with limited radar coverage. While recent generative flow matching models have greatly advanced retrieval quality, they face a critical trade-off: pixel-space formulations suffer from the prohibitive computational costs of attention-based U-Net velocity networks, whereas latent-space modeling often sacrifices fine precipitation details or struggles with sparse targets. To address this dilemma, we propose WaveOp-LiteFM, a lightweight neural operator flow matching framework for S2R retrieval. Our approach introduces a novel velocity backbone built upon the spectral-local-wavelet (SLW) block, enabling efficient and stable flow matching in pixel space. Specifically, the SLW block disentangles precipitation features into three distinct frequency regimes: (i) the spectral branch captures large-scale stratiform organization; (ii) the local branch models short-range interactions; and (iii) the wavelet branch enhances sharp structures while suppressing noisy high-frequency responses. Building on this design, an input-adaptive gating mechanism dynamically fuses features from the three functional branches. Furthermore, a skip gate efficiently reintegrates encoder features through additive fusion within the decoder, avoiding the costly channel concatenation used in conventional U-Net architectures. Experiments on the SEVIR and Southeast China datasets show that WaveOp-LiteFM achieves state-of-the-art retrieval performance while substantially reducing computational costs. Beyond benchmark evaluation, large-area inference over China, including a recent Typhoon Bavi case, demonstrates that WaveOp-LiteFM maintains reliable retrieval quality in large-scale real-world scenarios.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [203] [VLM-based automatic multi-granularity graph representation of building layouts for design informatics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24886)
*Song Guo,Zhuoshi Chen,Maosu Li,Weimin Zhuang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于视觉-语言模型（VLM）的自动构建建筑平面图多粒度图表示（Level-of-Graphs, LoGs）的方法，通过节点识别、边推断、文本解析和图粗化，将平面图图像转化为可用于设计检索、知识推理和BIM增强的结构化知识表示，并以147个全球学术图书馆平面图为案例进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑平面图蕴含丰富的功能空间关系知识，可用于设计检索、基于知识的推理及建筑全生命周期的BIM增强；然而，为公共建筑自动构建任务自适应的图表示仍具挑战性，是本研究要填补的空白。

Method: 首先定义公共建筑布局的多粒度Level-of-Graphs（LoGs）体系；随后提出基于VLM的自动LoG构建流程，包括节点识别、边推断、文本解析与图粗化；最后用147个全球学术图书馆平面图作为案例，对VLM生成的表示进行系统评估与真实任务测试。

Result: VLM生成的图与人工标注图高度一致（匹配节点比例>=92%；生成三种LoG图平均耗时509.3秒/平面图）。中等粒度图在节点级区域预测中表现最佳（Macro F1=0.647，计算复杂度仅为细粒度的65%）；粗粒度图在图级布局质量评估中最有效（Spearman's rho=0.610，复杂度仅为细粒度的16%）。

Conclusion: 本研究实现了从平面图图像到结构化知识表示的可扩展、免标注提取，推动了设计信息学的发展，增强了建筑全生命周期中设计信息的利用率。

Abstract: Architectural floorplan images encode rich relational knowledge among functional spaces, which underpins design retrieval, knowledge-based reasoning, and BIM enrichment through the building lifecycle. However, it remains challenging to automatically construct task-adaptive graph representations for public buildings. To address this gap, we first define a multi-granularity Level-of-Graphs (LoGs) for public building layouts. Methodologically, we present a Vision-Language Model (VLM)-based automatic LoG construction through node identification, edge inference, text parsing, and graph coarsening. VLM-generated representations are systematically evaluated and tested in real-world tasks, using 147 academic library floorplans worldwide as a case study. Experiments showed VLM-generated graphs were broadly consistent with human-labeled graphs (matched node ratio >= 92%; 509.3 s per floor plan for three-LoG graph generation). Meso-grained graphs yield the best node-level zone prediction (Macro F1 = 0.647, at 65% of fine-grained complexity), while coarse-grained graphs are most effective for graph-level layout quality evaluation (Spearman's \r{ho} = 0.610, at 16% of fine-grained complexity). By enabling scalable, annotation-free extraction of structured layout information from floorplan images, this study advances design informatics by converting plan images into knowledge representations, thereby enhancing the utilization of design information across the building life cycle.

</details>


### [204] [SIMGUIDE: Procedurally Grounded Multi-Context Representations for Personalized Agent Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24888)
*Chirag Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 SIMGUIDE 方法，将用户上下文组织为带类型、按领域划分的 Sims 块，并用以往决策的程序性示例进行 grounding；同时构建诊断基准 SIMBENCH（47 个偏好条件规划任务）。结果表明程序性 grounded Sims 在 GPT-4o 及 τ-bench 上均显著优于基于检索的个性化（RAG），且参数级 LoRA 微调与按 Sim 类型路由适配器同样印证：表示格式（而非表示内容）是首要设计变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化 AI 智能体把用户视为单一实体，将扁平档案拼接进提示词，无法处理同一人在不同生活场景中优先级不同甚至冲突的情况。作者指出核心问题并非信息缺失，而是用户表示的格式决定了智能体能否利用这些信息，因此需要重新设计用户上下文的表示结构。

Method: 提出 SIMGUIDE：将用户上下文结构化拆分为带类型的、领域特定的块（Sims），并用来自用户历史决策的程序性示例对每条约束进行 grounding；构建 SIMBENCH 诊断套件（47 个偏好条件规划任务），其正确计划取决于当前激活的用户上下文。在参数层面，采用任务匹配的 LoRA 微调，并按 Sim 类型而非用户身份进行适配器路由。

Result: 声明式 Sim 约束并不优于 RAG，但程序性 grounded Sims 在 GPT-4o 上较 RAG 提升 +7.9 Preference Adherence 点（p=0.013），且在 100 个 τ-bench 任务上于 GPT-4o 与 Claude Sonnet 4.5 中均复现（p≤0.023）。任务匹配的 LoRA 微调比未适配基座模型提升 12.8 ROUGE-L 点，按 Sim 类型而非用户身份路由适配器再增 7.3 点，且对 28% 的路由错误保持稳健。

Conclusion: 结论是表示格式——而非表示内容——是个性化智能体设计中第一优先级的变量：结构化、程序性 grounded 的用户表示与按语义类型组织的参数适配，能系统性超越现有“扁平档案 + 检索”范式，并在跨模型（GPT-4o、Claude Sonnet 4.5）上取得一致的统计显著收益。

Abstract: Personalized AI agents overwhelmingly treat users as single entities: a flat profile concatenated into a prompt. This fails when the same person holds different priorities across life contexts -- and fails catastrophically when those priorities conflict. The core problem is not that agents lack information about users; it is that the format of user representations determines whether an agent can act on that information at all. We introduce SIMGUIDE, a method that structures user context into typed, domain-specific blocks called Sims and grounds each constraint with procedural examples drawn from past decisions. To evaluate this, we construct SIMBENCH, a diagnostic suite of 47 preference-conditioned planning tasks where the correct plan depends on which user context is active -- a property no existing benchmark tests. Declarative Sim constraints alone do not outperform retrieval-based personalization (RAG). Procedurally grounded Sims outperform RAG on GPT-4o (+7.9 Preference Adherence points, $p = 0.013$), and this advantage replicates on 100 $τ$-bench tasks across both GPT-4o and Claude Sonnet~4.5 ($p \leq 0.023$). At the parametric level, the same principle holds: training distribution dominates whether parametric adaptation succeeds at all. Task-matched LoRA fine-tuning improves generation quality by 12.8 ROUGE-L points over the unadapted base model, and routing adapters by Sim type rather than user identity adds a further 7.3 points, robust to 28% routing error. Representation format -- not representation content -- is the first-order design variable.

</details>


### [205] [Reliable LLM-Powered Decision Engines for Large-Scale Supply Chain Operations: Architecture, Safety, and Performance Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24889)
*Nirmal Kumar Jingar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合架构——LLM赋能决策引擎（LLM-DE），将大语言模型与数学优化、概率预测及安全约束决策过滤相结合，用于大规模供应链的端到端决策（需求预测、库存优化、运输路径规划、中断缓解），在保留性能和安全性保证的同时实现比纯数据驱动或启发式方案更智能、更安全、更可扩展的决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模供应链高度不确定、动态且易受中断影响，传统基于规则或仅优化的系统难以提供及时且具有韧性的决策；而日益丰富的异构数据源（交易性需求信号与无结构的干扰报告）为构建能同时推理、适应和优化的智能系统提供了机遇。

Method: 提出LLM-DE混合架构：将LLM的语义推理能力与数学优化、概率预测及安全约束决策过滤集成，使语义推理具有一组性能与安全保证，从而支持大规模供应链中的安全决策；并提供完整的算法流水线和基于数学构造的形式化建模。

Result: 实验/论证结果表明，语言驱动的推理与优化及形式化约束相结合，能够产生不仅更智能，而且更安全、更具可扩展性的供应链决策，覆盖需求预测、库存优化、运输路由和中断缓解等端到端流程。

Conclusion: 该研究为引入LLM的运营决策系统提供了新架构、完整算法流水线和数学化表述，为下一代智能供应链基础设施奠定了兼具实用性与理论性的基础，可在高度不确定与大规模复杂性下可靠运行。

Abstract: Current large-scale supply chains are highly uncertain, dynamic, and disruption prone that are challenging to serve up timely and resilient decisions through traditional rule-based and optimization-only systems. The increasing supply of heterogeneous data sources, such as transactional demand signals and unstructured disruption report, presents a chance of intelligent systems, which could reason, adapt and optimize at the same time. A hybrid architecture that combines large language models (LLMs) with mathematical optimization, probabilistic forecasting, and safety-constrained decision filtering is proposed in this paper as a performance of a Decision Engine, which is called LLM-Powered Decision Engine (LLM-DE). In comparison to purely data-driven or heuristic solutions, LLM-DE integrates semantic reasoning with LLM with a set of performance and safety guarantees that allow safe decision-making in large-scale supply chain processes. The suggested framework enables the end-to-end decision making such as demand forecasting, inventory optimization, and transportation routing and disruption mitigation. The findings affirm that language-based reasoning combined with optimization and formal constraints can be used to come up with not only smarter but also safer and more scalable supply chain decisions. This research provides a new architecture, a complete pipeline of algorithm, and a formulation based on mathematical constructs of the operational decision systems incorporating LLM. The proposed model offers a pragmatic and theoretical basis of the next-generation intelligent supply chain infrastructures that can be implemented to work dependably in the face of uncertainty and massive complexity.

</details>


### [206] [PhysElite: How Far Are LLMs from Solving Olympiad-Level Physics Problems?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25097)
*Ruoran Xu,Wending Gao,Liyunfeng Chen,Aixin Shi,Haoyu Cheng,Zixiang Fang,Yiqiang Zou,Qiufeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PhysElite 是一个面向奥林匹克级物理推理的大规模双语（中英）多模态基准，包含 11,586 道高难度题目，每题配有视觉图、分步双语解答与最终答案。18 个开源与闭源 MLLM 的评测显示即使最强模型答案准确率也仅 33.7%，并做了步骤级过程评估以诊断模型推理链中的失败环节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理基准在两点上存在局限：（1）缺乏高难度数据集；（2）在视觉形式、知识点和分步解题过程上覆盖不全。因此模型在现有数据集上的表现不能充分代表其解决复杂物理问题的能力，需要更接近专家级物理推理的基准。

Method: 构建 PhysElite 大规模双语多模态基准，包含 11,586 道奥赛级题目，每道题提供视觉图、中英双语分步推导过程及最终答案；在此基础上评测 18 个开源与闭源多模态大语言模型（MLLM），并开展步骤级过程评估以定位失败环节。

Result: 即使最强的模型在答案准确率上也仅达到 33.7%，说明该基准难度显著高于现有数据集；18 个模型均表现欠佳，且通过分步过程评估揭示了模型在推理链中的具体失败位置。

Conclusion: PhysElite 填补了高难度、覆盖全面的专家级物理基准空白，表明当前 MLLM 在奥林匹克物理推理上仍有巨大差距，其数据集与步骤级评估可作为未来研究方向与模型改进的参考。

Abstract: Understanding how (multimodal) large language models perform on physics problems requires benchmarks that reflect the difficulty and breadth of expert-level physical reasoning. Existing physics benchmarks remain limited in the following two important ways: (1) short of high-difficulty datasets, and (2) lack of comprehensive coverage of visual forms, knowledge points, and step-by-step solution processes. As a result, model performance on current datasets may not be fully representative of their ability to solve complex physics problems. To address these issues, we present PhysElite, a large-scale bilingual multimodal benchmark for Olympiad-level physics reasoning. PhysElite contains 11,586 Olympiad-tier problems. For each problem, we provide corresponding visual diagrams, step-by-step bilingual Chinese-English solution derivations, and the final answer. We benchmark 18 open-source and closed-source MLLMs, and find that even the strongest model reaches only 33.7% answer accuracy. We additionally conduct step-level process evaluation to diagnose where models fail in the reasoning chain. Our datasets are released at https://huggingface.co/datasets/physelite/PhysElite.

</details>


### [207] [AI-Powered Mental Health Chatbots in Africa: A Systematic Review and Culturally Adaptive Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24890)
*Matshepo Lebese,Pitso Tsibolane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对2017–2025年间52项实证研究进行系统综述，评估AI心理聊天机器人在非洲情境下的文化、语言和基础设施适配性。结果发现其有改善可及性、缓解焦虑抑郁症状和扩大心理社会支持的潜力，但多数系统基于西方模式和英语设计，缺乏非洲本地化适配。作者据此提出文化适应性数字心理健康（CADMH）框架，整合非洲文化价值观、多语言设计、移动优先优化和伦理保障，并为研究、实践和政策提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非洲心理健康问题因基础设施不足、污名化和专业人员短缺而长期未得到充分解决，而AI聊天机器人作为低成本、高可及性的工具在全球兴起，但其在非洲语境下的文化、语言和基础设施适用性尚缺乏系统评估。

Method: 采用系统综述方法，选取2017至2025年间发表的52项实证研究，从文化、语言和基础设施三个维度对AI聊天机器人在非洲情境中的相关性进行批判性分析，并在此基础上构建CADMH理论框架。

Result: 研究发现AI聊天机器人能提升服务可及性、减轻焦虑和抑郁症状并扩展心理社会支持，但非洲本土化适配有限：现有系统大多沿用西方心理健康模型和英语界面，在本地相关性、包容性和可持续性方面存在关键缺口。

Conclusion: 作者提出融合非洲文化价值观、多语言设计、移动端优先优化与伦理保障的CADMH框架，明确了AI聊天机器人融入非洲医疗体系的机遇与障碍，为后续研究、临床实践和政策制定提供方向指引。

Abstract: Mental health challenges in Africa remain under-addressed due to inadequate infrastructure, stigma, and a chronic shortage of professionals. Artificial Intelligence (AI)-powered chatbots are emerging globally as low-cost, accessible tools that can offer psychological support. This paper presents a systematic review of 52 empirical studies published between 2017 and 2025, critically analysing their cultural, linguistic, and infrastructural relevance to African contexts. The findings demonstrate the potential of AI chatbots to improve accessibility, reduce symptoms of anxiety and depression, and expand psychosocial support, yet reveal limited African-specific adaptation. Most systems remain rooted in Western models and English-language designs, leaving critical gaps in local relevance, inclusivity, and sustainability. Based on the findings, the authors develop a Culturally Adaptive Digital Mental Health (CADMH) framework that integrates African cultural values, multilingual design, mobile-first optimisation, and ethical safeguards. The study highlights opportunities and barriers for integrating AI chatbots into African healthcare, offering guidance for research, practice, and policy.

</details>


### [208] [Measurement-Budget Allocation in Quantum Learning with Finite-Shot Generalization Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24891)
*Ferhat Ozgur Catak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对近端量子硬件上的有限测量预算分配问题，本文研究二进制量子分类器在"训练态数量 n"与"每个态的测量次数（shots）S"之间的权衡，给出分布无关的泛化界（样本项 ~ √(d/n)，shot 项 ~ √((log n)/S)），并通过保守闭式代理函数推导出最优分配规则 n*=2√(2dB/log(2B/δ))、S*=B/n*，对应 B^{-1/4} 的最坏情形速率；PennyLane 实验验证了理论界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近端量子硬件估计 Born 概率需要重复电路执行，而带有固定测量预算 B 的量子学习实验必须决定训练态数量 n 与每态分配 shots S 之间的权衡；已有的理论缺少对这一"样本数 vs 测量次数"权衡的明确刻画，因此需要在二进制量子分类器（理想分数为 Tr(Mρ)）框架下给出可操作的预算分配指导。

Method: 对使用固定或独立选择测量算子 M 的二进制量子分类器，推导一个分布无关（distribution-free）的泛化界，将有限样本项（√(d/n)）与有限 shot 项（√((log n)/S)）分离；在约束 B=nS 下两项反向变化，进而最小化该界的保守闭式代理函数，得到最优分配规则 n*=2√(2dB/log(2B/δ)) 与 S*=B/n*；并利用 PennyLane 在 2-qubit 和 4-qubit 变分量子电路上对九个合成二进制分类基准进行模拟验证。

Result: 代理函数与原精确极小化器具有相同渐近标度，给出最坏情形速率 B^{-1/4}；在所有测试配置中，单侧经验泛化间隙均低于理论界。该保证是刻意保守的，因为它适用于完整的二进制量子测量类别。

Conclusion: 该结果为有限 shot 评估与预实验规划提供了保守的统计预算分配准则，可作为硬件层面调度与电路设计考量的补充；将保证扩展到完全自适应的 shot 噪声训练仍是未解决的问题。

Abstract: On near-term quantum hardware, estimating a Born probability requires repeated circuit executions. A quantum learning experiment with a fixed measurement budget $B$ must therefore decide how many distinct training states $n$ to use and how many shots $S$ to allocate to each state. We study this tradeoff for binary quantum classifiers with fixed or independently selected measurement operators $M$, where the ideal score is $\Tr(Mρ)$. We prove a distribution-free generalization bound that separates the finite-sample and finite-shot contributions. The sample term scales as $\sqrt{d/n}$, while the shot term scales as $\sqrt{(\log n)/S}$; under the constraint $B=nS$, these two terms move in opposite directions. Minimising a conservative closed-form surrogate of the bound gives the allocation rule $\nstar = 2\sqrt{2dB/\log(2B/δ)}$ and $\Sstar = B/\nstar$. This surrogate has the same asymptotic scaling as the exact minimizer and yields a worst-case rate of $B^{-1/4}$. The guarantee is intentionally conservative, since it applies to the full class of binary quantum measurements. We complement the theory with PennyLane simulations using 2-qubit and 4-qubit variational quantum circuits on nine synthetic binary classification benchmarks. In all tested configurations, the one-sided empirical generalization gap remains below the theoretical bound. The result provides a conservative statistical guideline for allocating measurement budgets in finite-shot evaluation and pre-experimental planning for near-term quantum learning systems, complementing hardware-level scheduling and circuit-design considerations. Extending the guarantee to fully adaptive shot-noisy training remains an open problem.

</details>


### [209] [Natural Language Input, Semantic Track Representation, and LLM Inference: Making the Maritime Information Exchange Model Tractable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24892)
*Frederick Roth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种实用架构，利用当前大语言模型（LLM）技术使海事信息交换模型（MIEM）和更广泛的富语义轨道模型变得可行。核心思路是让操作员直接用自然语言贡献观测，由LLM将其翻译为类型化语义断言记录（SARs），知识图谱累积这些SARs，再由第二个LLM对图谱进行推理、异常检测和假设排序，从而消除学习形式化本体语言和手动编码逻辑断言的门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 国防和执法领域采用语义轨道模型的障碍在于，操作员必须学习形式化本体语言并手动将观测编码为类型化逻辑断言，这严重阻碍了模型的实际部署。作者希望消除这一障碍，使MIEM和Rich Semantic Track模型能够借助当前LLM技术立即投入应用。

Method: 架构采用两阶段LLM管道：第一阶段，操作员以自然语言输入观测，LLM将其转化为命名格框架（case frames）类型的语义断言记录（SARs），用以在单一紧凑结构中捕获n元关系；随后知识图谱累积SARs；第二阶段，另一个LLM在图谱上执行推理、异常检测和假设排序。文章通过两个详细示例（9/11时期袭击前指标场景和海事货物检查场景）展示从自然语言输入到SAR表示再到推理输出的完整流程。

Result: 通过两个详细示例验证了完整管道的可行性，论证该架构使Track Model和MIEM能够用当前技术立即部署，同时对专有的封闭实现建立了现有技术（prior art）主张，并将方法奠基于将语义轨道表示与神经流形几何联系起来的理论框架。

Conclusion: 该架构使语义轨道模型摆脱了形式化本体编码的人工负担，具备即用性，并兼具实际部署价值、防御性知识产权意义（建立先例以对抗专有封闭）和理论深度（与神经流形几何相关联）。

Abstract: We describe a practical architecture for making the Maritime Information Exchange Model (MIEM) and the broader Rich Semantic Track model tractable using current large language model (LLM) technology. The barrier to adoption of semantic track models in defense and law enforcement has been the requirement that operators learn formal ontology languages and manually encode observations as typed logical assertions. We propose eliminating this barrier entirely: operators contribute observations in natural language; an LLM translates these into typed Semantic Assertion Records (SARs), which are named case frames that capture n-ary relations in a single compact structure; a knowledge graph accumulates the SARs; and a second LLM pass performs inference, anomaly detection, and hypothesis ranking over the graph. We work through two detailed examples (a 9/11-era pre-attack indicator scenario and a maritime cargo inspection scenario) showing the full pipeline from natural language input to SAR representation to inference output. We argue that this architecture makes the Track Model and MIEM immediately deployable with current technology, establishes prior art against proprietary enclosure of the approach, and grounds the method in a theoretical framework connecting semantic track representations to neural manifold geometry.

</details>


### [210] [Same-Player Verification for Account Consistency in Counter-Strike 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24893)
*Xuchen Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于CS2比赛回放(demo)的“同玩家验证”(same-player verification)方法，用于账号完整性审查：将单场回放中一名玩家的行为轨迹编码为“演示玩家行为指纹”，并通过监督学习判断两条行为观测是否来自同一真实玩家。基于1,330场CS2 demo提取13,300条观测、构造663,590对样本，最终双样本模型平均ROC AUC达0.931（95%精确率下不同玩家召回率0.722），而通过多场历史聚合可将AUC提升至0.986。特征分析表明，准星控制、开火节奏、移动-停火协调等低层机械习惯比单场绩效更能提供身份信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在CS2等竞技FPS游戏中，账号完整性审查常需判断某账号近期行为是否与其历史操作者一致，典型场景包括账号临时替换、代练(rank boosting)和高水平玩家使用低段位账号(smurfing)。人工审查需将当前对战与多场历史对战逐一比对，成本高且主观，因此需要一个自动化的“同玩家验证”框架。

Method: 首先将单名玩家在一场demo中的行为轨迹编码为统一的“演示玩家行为指纹”，指纹基于CS2游戏理解设计，覆盖五个维度：准星控制、移动-停火配合、经济/购买、交火/交战、时间节奏。随后训练监督模型判断两条行为观测是否来自同一真实玩家（二分类对样本）。数据规模：从1,330场demo提取13,300条演示玩家观测，在88.4M候选对空间中抽样出663,590个同/异玩家对用于训练与评估；并加入K场历史聚合的账号级一致性命中建模。

Result: 双样本配对模型平均ROC AUC为0.931，在95%精确率约束下达到0.722的不同玩家召回率。特征分析显示最强的身份信号来自低层操作，尤其准星控制、开火节奏和移动-停火协调，表明稳定的低层机械习惯比单场绩效结果更具区分力。账号历史聚合评估中，历史深度从K=1的0.931提升到K=10的0.986，验证了多demo历史聚合的有效性。

Conclusion: CS2 demo行为可支撑有监督的同玩家验证，并通过多场历史聚合实现账号级身份一致性建模。低层机械操作习惯是身份判别的主要依据，账号历史深度可显著提升验证精度，为账号完整性审查（如代练、换人、炸鱼检测）提供了可行的自动化方案。

Abstract: In competitive first-person shooter (FPS) games such as Counter-Strike 2 (CS2), account-integrity review often asks whether an account's recent behavior remains consistent with its historical operator. This consistency question arises in cases such as temporary substitution, rank boosting, and high-skill players using lower-ranked accounts, where manual review requires comparing a current match against multiple historical matches. We formulate this review task as same-player verification: we encode the behavioral trajectory of a single player in a match replay (demo) as a demo-player behavioral fingerprint, and train a model to judge whether two behavioral observations come from the same real player. Grounded in CS2 game understanding, the fingerprints cover crosshair control, movement-stop-fire coordination, economy/buy, combat/engagement, and temporal rhythm. From 1,330 CS2 demos we extract 13,300 demo-player observations, and sample 663,590 same/different pairs from an 88.4M candidate-pair space for supervised training and evaluation. The final pairwise model reaches an average ROC AUC of 0.931 and achieves 0.722 different-player recall at 95% precision. Feature analysis shows that the strongest identity signals come from low-level operations, especially crosshair control, firing rhythm, and movement-stop-fire coordination, indicating that stable low-level mechanical habits are more informative for this verification task than single-match performance outcomes. In the account-history aggregation evaluation, increasing history depth raises AUC from the K=1 single-pair baseline of 0.931 to 0.986 at K=10. These results show that CS2 demo behavior can support supervised same-player verification and account-level identity-consistency modeling through multi-demo history aggregation.

</details>


### [211] [Semantic Graph Unification for Industrial Digital Threads: Bridging 11 Heterogeneous Manufacturing Systems Through Ontology-Driven Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24918)
*Grama Chethan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向工业数字线程的开源语义图统一框架，通过本体驱动的RDF知识图谱将ERP、MES、PLM、SCADA等11个异构数据源（覆盖9个领域）经五阶段ETL流水线整合，并以97条owl:sameAs链接实现自动实体解析。框架包含78个RDFS类、108个对象属性、243个数据属性，并融合ISA-95、OPC UA、eClass、AAS、RAMI 4.0等标准。自动化发现引擎基于九类策略（跨站关联、告警覆盖、ECN影响、CUSUM/EWMA漂移检测等）跨系统边界挖掘洞察。核心实证结论：阻断24个跨系统工具后召回率从1.00降至0.31（F1从1.00降至0.48），表明69%的可发现信号依赖跨系统图连接；留一法消融证实9类策略中6类贡献独有信号；在65个信号清单（16正/49空）上达到F1=1.00（95% Clopper-Pearson CI [0.79, 1.00]），但清单由作者自建，属于验证而非独立校验。图谱通过287个Model Context Protocol工具以SPARQL原生语义层暴露给LLM智能体，并在航空航天、快消品、制药、医疗器械、涡轮叶片五个行业模板中验证了模式的跨行业稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代制造企业运行ERP、MES、PLM、SCADA、QMS、SCM等异构系统，各自拥有独立数据模型与API，形成数据孤岛，阻碍整体分析、延迟根本原因调查，并妨碍工业4.0的可追溯性。点对点集成复杂度呈O(n^2)增长且累积脆弱依赖，亟需一种可扩展、语义统一的整合方案。

Method: 方法包含四层：(1) 本体驱动的RDF知识图谱，融合ISA-95、OPC UA、eClass、AAS、RAMI 4.0等标准，涵盖78个RDFS类、108个对象属性、243个数据属性；(2) 五阶段ETL流水线，统一11个模拟数据源（9个领域）并借助97条owl:sameAs链接实现自动实体解析；(3) 自动化发现引擎，应用九类策略（跨站相关、告警覆盖、ECN影响、CUSUM/EWMA漂移检测等）跨系统边界挖掘信号；(4) 将图谱作为SPARQL原生语义层，通过287个Model Context Protocol工具暴露给LLM智能体。

Result: 主要实证结果：(a) 阻断24个跨系统工具后召回率由1.00降至0.31、F1由1.00降至0.48，证明69%的可发现信号需要跨系统图连接；(b) 留一法消融表明九类策略中六类贡献了独有信号；(c) 在65信号清单（16正/49空）上F1=1.00（95% CI [0.79, 1.00]），但清单由作者构建，构成验证（verification）而非独立验证（validation）；(d) 五个行业模板（航空航天、CPG、制药、医疗器械、涡轮叶片）展示模式跨行业稳定。

Conclusion: 语义图统一框架可有效打破制造企业数据孤岛：跨系统图连接是工业洞察的核心来源（阻断后损失69%信号），本体驱动的方法能在多行业间保持模式稳定，并能以SPARQL语义层向LLM智能体开放。同时作者坦诚指出，由于信号清单为自建，当前结果为验证性证据，仍需独立外部验证以确立普适性。

Abstract: Modern manufacturing enterprises operate heterogeneous systems -- ERP, MES, PLM, SCADA, QMS, SCM -- each with its own data model and API. The resulting silos prevent holistic analysis, delay root-cause investigation, and obstruct Industry 4.0 traceability. Point-to-point integration scales as O(n^2) and accumulates brittle dependencies. This paper presents an open framework for semantic graph unification of industrial digital threads. An ontology-driven RDF knowledge graph unifies data from 11 simulated sources across nine domains through a five-stage ETL pipeline with automated entity resolution spanning 97 owl:sameAs identity links. The ontology encompasses 78 RDFS classes, 108 object properties, and 243 data properties, drawing on ISA-95, OPC UA, eClass, the Asset Administration Shell, RAMI 4.0, and additional standards. An automated discovery engine applies nine strategy categories -- cross-station correlation, alarm coverage, ECN impact, CUSUM/EWMA drift detection -- to surface insights spanning system boundaries. The primary empirical result: blocking 24 cross-system tools reduces recall from 1.00 to 0.31 (F1 from 1.00 to 0.48), showing that 69% of discoverable signals require cross-system graph joins. Leave-one-out ablation confirms six of nine strategies contribute unique signals. Verification against a 65-signal manifest (16 positive, 49 null) yields F1 = 1.00 (95% Clopper-Pearson CI [0.79, 1.00]); as the manifest was author-constructed, this constitutes verification not independent validation. The graph is exposed to LLM agents via 287 Model Context Protocol tools as a SPARQL-native semantic layer. Five industry templates (aerospace, CPG, pharma, medical devices, turbine blades) demonstrate schema stability across manufacturing verticals.

</details>


### [212] [post-graph-rag: A PostgreSQL-Native Graph RAG Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24921)
*Chandan Rajah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: post-graph-rag 是一个开源图检索增强生成（Graph RAG）引擎，针对现有实现的三大缺陷进行改进：通过将所有组件（文本块、嵌入、实体图、社区摘要）整合进单一 PostgreSQL 数据库（pgvector 负责向量搜索，边表负责遍历）来消除基础设施复杂度；通过提取期不变量拒绝模糊谓词、代词名、裸数量等低质量输出，并支持谓词规范化与实体消歧来提升图质量；通过时间层（有效性期间、文档顺序冲突消解、as-of 查询）处理事实随时间演变的问题。在相同提取与嵌入模型下对比 LightRAG，构建了更密集、更易查询的图，查询延迟更低，并支持基线不具备的时间演化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图 RAG 实现存在三重代价：基础设施上需同时维护向量库、图数据库和文档库并保持三者一致；图质量上，不拒绝输出的提取管线在图中填满了不表达任何事实的边；时间维度上，只增不减的图将过时事实与当前事实等同对待。作者旨在用一个引擎同时解决这三个问题。

Method: 核心方法是工程化整合而非引入新模型：(1) 存储整合——文本块与嵌入、规范化实体图和社区摘要全部存放在单个 PostgreSQL 数据库中，用 pgvector 做搜索、边表做遍历；(2) 提取期不变量（在任何写入前运行）——拒绝模糊谓词、代词化名称和裸数量；谓词按可选词表规范化；通过模型提供的别名将实体解析到每个规范化名一个顶点；被否认的关系以否定标记保留肯定谓词；(3) 时间层——关系可携带来自文本的有效期，允许后续文档仅凭文档顺序取代先前不兼容的断言，并支持 as-of 时间点查询。

Result: 在三个语料库上、使用相同提取与嵌入模型对比 LightRAG：post-graph-rag 在所有数据集上构建出更密集的图，每实体的关系数最高达 LightRAG 的 2.4 倍；图更易查询——不同边标签数与关系数的比值在 0.46–0.58（受控词表下为 0.11），对比 LightRAG 的 0.77–1.33；回答质量相当且查询延迟更低；支持基线不具备的时间演化——在一个新序列和十年的申报文件中分别有 13 和 8 个关系被取代，而基线为 0。作者明确强调这些是工程测量而非基准测试结果。

Conclusion: 作者认为通过工程整合与约束式提取，可以同时消除图 RAG 的基础设施碎片化、图污染和时态僵化三大问题。post-graph-rag 证明单一数据库配合提取期不变量与时间层即可达到更具可查询性、更密集且支持时间演化的图，并不牺牲回答质量。同时作者保持谨慎，指明结果是工程测量而非基准成绩，并提供开源代码供复现与进一步验证。

Abstract: Graph-based retrieval-augmented generation connects facts that no single passage states, but current implementations pay for that three times: in infrastructure, requiring a vector store, graph database and document store to be kept consistent; in graph quality, because an extraction pipeline that never refuses output fills the graph with edges that assert nothing; and over time, because a graph that only accumulates treats superseded and current facts alike.
  post-graph-rag is an open-source engine addressing all three. Text chunks with embeddings, a canonical entity graph and community summaries live in one PostgreSQL database, with pgvector for search and edge tables for traversal. Extraction-time invariants run before anything is written: vague predicates, pronominal names and bare quantities are rejected; predicates are normalised onto an optional vocabulary; entities resolve to one vertex per canonical name via model-supplied aliases; and denied relations keep the positive predicate under a negation flag. A temporal layer lets relations carry a validity period from the prose, lets a later document supersede an earlier incompatible assertion from document order alone, and answers as-of queries.
  Against LightRAG on three corpora under identical extraction and embedding models, post-graph-rag builds a denser graph everywhere, up to $2.4\times$ the relations per entity, and a more queryable one: distinct edge labels run at 0.46 to 0.58 per relation, 0.11 under a controlled vocabulary, against 0.77 to 1.33. It answers comparably with lower query latency, and supports temporal evolution the baseline lacks: 13 and 8 relationships superseded on a novel sequence and a decade of filings, against zero. These are engineering measurements, not a benchmark result.
  Code: https://github.com/crajah/post-graph-rag, https://github.com/crajah/post-graph

</details>


### [213] [FrontierChallenge: Evaluating Scientific Workflow Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24979)
*Liangcai Su,Zhaopeng Feng,Zhuo Chen,Zhen Zhang,Xiang Lin,Ruilin Li,Handuo Zhang,Ning Wang,Kailong Wen,Yueqi Guo,Feng Xing,Yiling Guo,Chenxiong Qian,Simon Shaolei Du,Lidong Bing,Xinyu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍FrontierChallenge，一个跨领域科学智能体基准，包含97个已发布、300个总体的端到端科学工作流任务（覆盖量子化学、分子动力学、材料表征、分析化学、生命科学、电化学/环境等领域）。评估了12个前沿模型与3种智能体脚手架。最佳配置仅完成97个任务中的20个（通过率20.6%）；部分任务平均分很高（如分析化学87.6、电化学/环境94.9）但通过率极低（最高4%和0%）；Claude Code未通过轨迹中75.5%仍以声称完成的语言结尾。这表明高部分得分和自信的完成声明均不能可靠地表明任务已完整交付，需结合端到端工作流执行与科学交付物完整性进行联合评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学智能体基准大多只强调最终答案、孤立程序或单一领域，无法反映智能体实际分析数据、执行代码并产出研究工件的端到端能力。因此需要构建一个跨领域、以完整科学交付物为目标的基准，来真实衡量科学智能体的完整任务完成能力。

Method: 构建FrontierChallenge基准：包含300个端到端科学工作流，本文发布并评估其中97个任务，覆盖量子化学、分子动力学、材料表征、分析化学、生命科学、电化学/环境六个领域。每个任务提供固定输入，并规定一组必需的科学交付物。评估12个前沿模型×3种智能体脚手架。采用Pass Rate（满足全完成标准的任务比例）衡量完全完成，Avg. Score衡量部分进展。

Result: 所有最佳配置仅完成97个发布任务中的20个，Pass Rate为20.6%。部分进展到完整交付的转化在分析化学和电化学/环境中最差：Avg. Score分别达到87.6和94.9，但最高Pass Rate仅4%和0%。在Claude Code未通过的轨迹中，75.5%仍以语言上声称完成而结束。

Conclusion: 高部分得分或自信的完成声明都不能可靠地说明科学任务已被完整交付。有必要同时评估端到端工作流执行过程与科学交付物的完整性，以避免被部分进展和虚假完成声明误导。

Abstract: Scientific agents increasingly analyze data, execute code, and produce research artifacts, yet most benchmarks emphasize final answers, isolated programs, or a single domain. We introduce FrontierChallenge, a cross-domain benchmark comprising 300 end-to-end scientific workflows. In this paper, we release and evaluate 97 of these tasks, spanning quantum chemistry, molecular dynamics, materials characterization, analytical chemistry, life science, and electrochemistry/environment. Each task provides fixed inputs and specifies a bundle of required scientific deliverables. We evaluate twelve frontier models with three agent scaffolds. Pass Rate measures the fraction of tasks satisfying the full-completion criterion, while Avg. Score captures partial progress. Each of the best-performing configurations completed only 20 of the 97 released tasks, yielding a Pass Rate of 20.6%. Partial progress translated especially poorly into complete delivery in analytical chemistry and electrochemistry/environment: Avg. Scores reached 87.6 and 94.9, but the highest Pass Rates were only 4% and 0%. Among non-passing Claude Code trajectories, 75.5% still ended with language claiming completion. These findings show that neither high partial scores nor confident claims of completion reliably indicate that a scientific task has been fully delivered, highlighting the need to evaluate end-to-end workflow execution and the completeness of scientific deliverables together.

</details>


### [214] [Solving Robust POMDPs with Omega-regular Objectives via Partially Observable Stochastic Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24986)
*Durgam Latha,Dion Reji,S. Akshay,Djordje Zikelic,Shankaranarayanan Krishna*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有一般ω-正则目标（涵盖可达性、安全性、LTL等）的鲁棒部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（RPOMDP）的求解问题。作者证明了在(s,a)-矩形且不确定性集为多面体的RPOMDP与部分可观测随机博弈（POSG）之间可以双向归约，首次建立了二者的语义等价性，并据此推导出求解RPOMDP及RMDP的一批新的计算复杂性上下界结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典POMDP假设转移概率完全已知，而RPOMDP将其推广到转移概率仅属于某个不确定性集的场景，更具现实意义。然而RPOMDP在一般ω-正则目标下的求解问题此前缺乏完整的理论刻画，尤其缺少与POSG之间等价性的系统研究。

Method: 作者针对(s,a)-矩形且不确定性集为多面体的RPOMDP，证明了其在ω-正则目标下的求解问题可以归约为POSG在相同目标下的求解问题；同时首次证明了反向归约亦成立，从而建立了两个模型类之间的语义等价性。

Result: 通过双向归约建立了RPOMDP与POSG的语义等价性；基于此推导出求解RPOMDP在不同ω-正则目标下的一系列新的计算复杂性结果（包括上界和下界）；作为推论，还为RMDP得到了新的计算复杂性结论。

Conclusion: RPOMDP（(s,a)-矩形多面体不确定性集）与POSG在语义上等价，这一桥梁使得POSG领域的算法与复杂性结果可以迁移到RPOMDP上，为RPOMDP在ω-正则目标下的求解提供了完整的复杂性刻画，并顺带改进了RMDP的复杂性结果。

Abstract: Robust POMDPs (RPOMDPs) generalize classical POMDPs to the setting where exact transition probabilities are not known -- rather, they are only known to belong to some uncertainty set of values. In this work, we study the problem of solving RPOMDPs with general omega-regular objectives, which subsume a broad class of objectives such as reachability, safety, and linear temporal logic (LTL) objectives. We show that, for (s,a)-rectangular RPOMDPs with polytopic uncertainty sets, the problem of solving RPOMDPs under omega-regular objectives can be reduced to solving partially observable stochastic games (POSGs) under omega-regular objectives. Moreover, we show for the first time that reductions can be constructed in both directions, establishing the semantic equivalence between (s,a)-rectangular RPOMDPs with polytopic uncertainty sets and POSGs. This allows us to derive a range of new computational complexity results, including both upper and lower complexity bounds, on solving RPOMDPs with different omega-regular objectives. As a corollary, we also derive new computational complexity results for RMDPs.

</details>


### [215] [CVE-SAI: Counterfactual Visual Evidence-Guided Selective Attribute Indexing for Risk-Controlled E-commerce Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25023)
*Xiaolong Sun,Qichao Wang,Hangyu Li,Liang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 CVE-SAI（反事实视觉证据引导的选择性属性索引）框架，将“推理候选值”与“是否准入索引”两个环节解耦：先基于主图像与属性问题冻结一个受本体约束的候选值，再通过反事实干预与证据引导的注意力重分配来审计该候选的视觉支撑性，最后在5%不安全准入预算下进行族级校准后决定是否写入持久索引。在 Amazon Berkeley Objects 的五个视觉属性上，该方法在属性推理、证据定位、认证准入覆盖率与受控检索性能上均优于自动准入基线，并显著降低不安全自动引入暴露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态产品模型在补全缺失电商属性时仍以属性答案准确率为优化目标，不验证视觉支撑；同时将瞬态预测与持久索引准入混为一谈，缺乏对事实错误或视觉无支撑值的显式风险控制，导致不可靠值污染持久索引。

Method: CVE-SAI 先在不依赖目录文本的情况下，从主图像和属性问题中推理并冻结本体约束的候选值；随后用 Focus-Zone Distortion（FZD）通过受控反事实干预构造属性特定的视觉依赖代理，再用 Evidence-Guided Attention Redistribution（EGAR）以该代理细化本体约束打分。候选在证据必要性、证据保留、干扰变换稳定性、候选相关目录文本冲突等审计之前保持冻结，目录文本只能收紧准入而不能修改候选；最后通过独立族级校准在5%不安全准入预算下选择一个策略并给出同时一阶有限样本界。

Result: 在 Amazon Berkeley Objects 的五个视觉属性上，CVE-SAI 提升了属性推理与证据定位能力；在共享风险协议下取得最高的认证准入覆盖率；在自动准入系统中取得最强的受控检索性能，并具有最低的不安全自动引入暴露。

Conclusion: 将推断与准入解耦可使具有视觉支撑的属性补全提升检索效果，同时限制持久索引污染，为电商多模态属性索引提供显式风险可控的范式。

Abstract: Multimodal product models can complete missing e-commerce attributes, yet current methods still optimize attribute-answer accuracy without verifying visual support, conflate transient prediction with persistent index admission, and lack explicit risk control over factually incorrect or visually unsupported values. We address these gaps with Counterfactual Visual Evidence-Guided Selective Attribute Indexing (CVE-SAI), which first infers and freezes an ontology-constrained candidate from the primary image and attribute question without catalog text, and then decides whether that candidate should enter the index. Focus-Zone Distortion (FZD) constructs an attribute-specific visual-dependence proxy through a controlled counterfactual intervention, and Evidence-Guided Attention Redistribution (EGAR) uses the proxy to refine ontology-constrained scoring. The canonical candidate is frozen before evidence necessity, evidence retention, nuisance-transformation stability, and candidate-specific catalog-text conflict audits; catalog text can only tighten admission and cannot revise the candidate. Independent family-level calibration selects one policy with a simultaneous one-sided finite-sample bound under a 5% unsafe-admission budget. Experiments on five visual attributes derived from Amazon Berkeley Objects show that CVE-SAI improves attribute inference and evidence localization, achieves the highest certified admission coverage under the shared risk protocol, and yields the strongest controlled retrieval performance with the lowest unsafe auto-induced exposure among automatic-admission systems. Separating inference from admission therefore enables visually supported attribute completion to improve retrieval while limiting persistent index contamination.

</details>


### [216] [Retrieve, Match, Escalate: Accurate and Scalable Product Linking with VLM-Distilled Cross-Encoders and Agentic VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25037)
*Jian Wang,Steven Xu,Sanjyot Thete,Maryam Barouti,Tom Tang,Elaine Wu,Charu Sareen,Kyle MacDonald*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一套面向大规模电商市场的"检索-匹配"级联生产系统，按难度分配计算资源：检索召回候选，轻量文本交叉编码器自动解决高置信度多数样本，由自托管开源多模态VLM代理处理歧义尾部样本（可查看商品图片并联网搜索证据）。交叉编码器从数百万双VLM共识标签中蒸馏而来，无需人工标注，并以98%精确率门槛校准自动接受；代理模型无需微调即达到闭源前沿VLM的精度，召回率88%对92%，单对成本约为其七分之一。单对成本跨越近五个数量级，仅将困难尾部升级到代理即可将端到端链接覆盖率从68%提升至77%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 产品链接（将商家商品记录映射到规范化目录产品的实体消解任务）在平台级规模上面临巨大挑战：数十亿条嘈杂的多品类记录需与数千万个规范化产品匹配。单一模型对所有候选打分时，要么难以处理困难样本（模型太弱），要么对简单样本成本过高（模型太贵），因此需要按难度自适应分配计算资源的方案。

Method: 提出生产级"检索-匹配"级联流水线：(1) 检索阶段召回可能的匹配；(2) 轻量文本交叉编码器自动解析高置信度的大多数样本，该编码器从数百万个"双VLM共识"标签蒸馏训练，彻底移除人类标注，并以98%精确率标准（经小型运营方认证审计验证）校准自动接受；(3) 代理式多模态VLM处理模糊剩余样本，通过检查商品图片和发起网络搜索获取记录中不存在的证据。代理采用自托管开源权重模型，无需微调。

Result: 级联各阶段的单对成本跨越近五个数量级（从廉价交叉编码器到前沿VLM）。自托管开源代理模型达到闭源前沿VLM的精确率，召回率为88%对92%，单对成本约为后者的七分之一。仅将困难尾部样本升级到代理模型，端到端链接覆盖率即从廉价阶段的68%提升至77%。

Conclusion: 验证了"按难度成比例投入计算"的级联设计在生产规模上的有效性：通过蒸馏VLM共识标签消除人工标注瓶颈、以精确率校准实现无人值守自动接受，并借助开源代理模型以约七分之一成本逼近前沿模型精度，实现成本与覆盖率的最优平衡，为电商平台级实体消解提供了可落地的架构范式。

Abstract: Product linking, the entity-resolution task of mapping merchant product records to canonical catalog products, consolidates fragmented listings so downstream search, recommendation, and advertising see one clean entry per product. At marketplace scale, billions of noisy, multi-category records must be resolved against tens of millions of canonical products, where scoring every candidate with a single model is either too weak for the hard cases or too costly for the easy ones. We present a production retrieve-then-match cascade that spends computation in proportion to difficulty: retrieval surfaces plausible matches, a lightweight text cross-encoder auto-resolves the high-confidence majority, and an agentic multimodal vision-language model settles the ambiguous remainder by inspecting product images and issuing web searches for evidence that is in neither record. The cross-encoder is distilled from millions of dual-VLM-consensus labels, retiring human annotation from the training set, and is calibrated to auto-accept links at a 98% precision bar validated against a smaller operator-certified audit. The agent is a self-hosted open-weight model that reaches a closed frontier VLM's precision at a four-point recall cost (88% versus 92%) for roughly one-seventh the per-pair cost, with no fine-tuning. Per-pair cost spans nearly five orders of magnitude from the cheap cross-encoder to the frontier VLM, so escalating only the hard tail to the agent raises end-to-end link coverage from the cheap stage's 68% to 77%.

</details>


### [217] [LifePlanner: Evaluating LLM Agents for Geo-spatial Planning with Social Media Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25039)
*Zhen Dong,Yuning Peng,Yutao Shi,Lei Zhong,Yongsen Mao,Yuan Liu,Haiping Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LifePlanner 是一个地理空间规划基准测试，将地图数据与大规模本地社交媒体帖子相结合，并通过 MCP 工具集提供访问。实验表明前沿 LLM 在简单检索上表现良好，但在复杂规划任务上性能急剧下降（Pass Rate 降至 40.2%），失败主因是证据获取不完整、工具使用不精确和约束整合薄弱，而非模型规模或推理长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地理空间规划（如旅行设计）是 LLM 智能体的现实测试场景，因为它需要基于地面的工具使用、嘈杂的证据检索和多约束推理。然而大多数现有基准只提供干净的地理空间数据和工具，缺少人们在日常规划中使用的开放式社会信号，因此现有评估无法反映真实世界的规划复杂性。

Method: LifePlanner 通过用大规模本地社交媒体帖子丰富地图数据，并提供基于 MCP（Model Context Protocol）工具集的访问来构建基准。该基准包含一个评估套件，涵盖四个任务类别和三个难度级别。

Result: 实验结果显示，前沿 LLM 在简单检索任务上表现良好，但在复杂规划任务上性能急剧下降，Pass Rate 降至 40.2%。失败主要源于从大规模多模态数据库中获取证据不完整、工具使用不精确以及约束整合薄弱，这些与模型规模或推理长度无关。

Conclusion: 未来的进展需要有效的基于地面的规划能力（grounded planning），而不是仅依靠模型规模的扩展。

Abstract: Geo-spatial planning, like trip design, is a realistic testbed for LLM agents because it requires grounded tool use, noisy evidence retrieval, and multi-constraint reasoning. Most benchmarks, however, only provide clean geospatial data and tools, missing the open-ended social signals that people use in daily planning. We introduce LifePlanner, a benchmark that enriches map data with large-scale local social media posts and provides access through an MCP toolset. LifePlanner provides an evaluation suite spanning four task categories and three difficulty levels. Experiments show frontier LLMs perform well on simple retrieval but degrade sharply on complex planning, with the Pass Rate dropping to 40.2%. Results show that failures mainly stem from incomplete evidence acquisition from such a large multimodal database, imprecise tool use, and weak constraint integration rather than model size or reasoning length, suggesting that future progress requires effective grounded planning instead of scaling alone.

</details>


### [218] [SimVerity: When Does Simulated Agent Success Survive Physical Deployment?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25067)
*Zhonghao Zhan,Yefan Zhang,Krinos Li,Hamed Haddadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 SimVerity，一个用于智能家居 AI 智能体"判定可迁移性"的保障框架：通过在同一目标部署环境中回放匹配场景，并将智能体的执行结果与物理世界中有资质验证者的观测进行交叉验证。实验表明，部署成功是一个真实世界过程而非模拟中的静态属性——完成度、报告状态、可观察效果与最终稳定状态在同一执行中会发生分歧；先进模拟器虽通过全部 240 个灯光测试，但摄像头捕获了 42 个对稳定状态检查不可见的亚秒级失败。该框架还可预先学习风险剖面以预测未实测路径上的失败，并量化智能体的可审计性。结论是 SimVerity 将判定迁移变成显式的"放行、弃权或升级"决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟评估被广泛用于基准测试 AI 智能体，但模拟通过所提供的证据能在多大程度上预测物理部署成功率，尚未被系统量化。作者旨在填补这一空白，并指出模拟器可能产生"虚假放行"（false clearance）。

Method: SimVerity 框架：(1) 在目标智能家居部署上重放匹配场景；(2) 将智能体执行与独立资质认证的物理见证者（如摄像头）进行交叉验证；(3) 从实测试验中学习风险剖面并锁定，用于预测未经物理测量的路径上的失败；(4) 通过切换智能体循环的模型客户端/服务配置来测量可审计性；(5) 对比第二个有资质的模拟器以检验其独立交叉检验价值。

Result: 部署成功是一个真实世界过程：完成、报告状态、可观察效果和稳定结果在同一执行内出现分歧；先进模拟器通过全部 240 个灯光试验，但摄像头捕获 42 个亚秒级失败（稳定状态检查不可见）。基于实测学习的风险剖面能在其从未物理测量的路径上预测失败，在 11 个保留会话中（两个队列）均优于属性无关的基线。切换一个智能体循环的模型客户端/服务配置，将其场景匹配份额从 52-88% 提升至 100%。第二个合格模拟器在重叠案例上从未出现分歧，未提供任何独立交叉检验；只有物理测量暴露了共同的盲点。

Conclusion: 模拟通过并不等同于部署成功；物理测量是识别模拟器共同盲点的必要条件。SimVerity 把判定迁移转化为部署前的显式决策：放行（clear）、弃权（abstain）或升级（escalate），从而为智能体部署提供可量化的保障与审计途径。

Abstract: Simulated evaluation is widely used to benchmark AI agents, yet how much evidence a simulated pass provides about physical deployment has not been systematically quantified. We present SimVerity, a verdict-transfer assurance framework: it replays matched scenarios on target smart home deployments and cross-validates agent execution against independently qualified physical witnesses. Our evaluation highlights that deployment success is a real-world process, not a static property in simulation: completion, reported state, observable effect, and settled outcome diverged within the same execution. Although an advanced simulator cleared all 240 light trials, a camera caught 42 sub-second failures invisible to settled-state checks. False clearance was predictable: a risk profile learned from measured trials and locked before evaluation predicted failures on a path it never physically measured, beating a property-blind baseline in all eleven held-out sessions across two cohorts. Agent auditability was also measurable: switching one agent loop's model-client/serving configuration raised its scenario-matching share from 52-88% to 100%. Finally, a second qualified simulator added no independent cross-check: it never disagreed on any overlapping case, and only physical measurement exposed their shared blind spots. SimVerity turns verdict transfer into an explicit decision: clear, abstain, or escalate before deployment.

</details>


### [219] [Auto-Policy, not Auto-Skill: Compiled Agent Skills for the Physical World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25091)
*Zhonghao Zhan,Hamed Haddadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出当前自进化技能体系（如 AutoSkills、Hermes Agent）所宣称的收益仅是效率而非安全，并定义了一类尚未被报道的攻击——"Borrowed Authority"（借用权限）：恶意或误用的代理技能可通过在技能制品中附加权限声明来驱动物理执行（如开门、转账），而接收方缺乏类型化的拒绝机制。作者提出 Edge Skillguard，一种内嵌于技能制品本身、而非像工作流引擎那样位于工具之间的类型化权限层，基于世界状态和传感器证据进行守卫。在真实边缘控制面测试平台上，该方案在五种攻击变体下 60/60 拦截所有借用权限请求且不阻断良性请求，并在 5 倍规模与 Tailscale 网格的多主机场景下保持有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自进化技能系统自动生成更多咨询式编排，但其报告收益集中在效率而非安全，这忽略了真正的鸿沟：技能（Skill）描述代理"应如何行为"，策略（Policy）决定哪些行为"被允许变为动作"。当前格式只覆盖前者（通过 markdown 和脚本），后者则完全交给模型自行判断，因此生成更多技能扩大的是鸿沟而非安全性。已有两类邻近攻击被记录：恶意技能危害云软件、被越狱的 LLM 控制机器人造成物理伤害；两者的交集——恶意代理技能造成物理伤害——虽直接可得却无人报告，这正是本文要填补的安全空白。

Method: 作者将上述攻击类别命名为 Borrowed Authority：技能格式使接收方代理没有任何类型化手段来拒绝跨代理的权限声明，恶意或被滥用的技能可通过附加声明驱动执行器。据此提出 Edge Skillguard——一个类型化权限层，它存在于技能制品内部（而非像工作流引擎那样位于工具之间），对世界状态和传感器证据施加守卫。实现与验证基于一个真实的边缘控制平面测试平台，并扩展至 5 倍规模和 Tailscale 网格多主机环境。

Result: 在真实边缘控制平面测试平台上，守卫在五种攻击变体下拦截了 60/60 的借用权限请求，且不阻断任何良性请求；该结果在 5 倍规模以及跨主机（Tailscale 网格）场景下依然成立。

Conclusion: 结果表明，高风险技能应将类型化调用策略与过程性知识共同打包，使物理动作依赖于机器可检查的证据而非对等代理的声明，从而以工程化手段弥合"行为规范"与"执行授权"之间的安全鸿沟。

Abstract: Self-evolving Skill harnesses (AutoSkills, Hermes Agent) generate more advisory orchestration automatically; their reported gains are efficiency, not safety. This misses the actual gap: a Skill describes how an agent should behave; a Policy decides which behavior is allowed to become an action. Today's format covers the first with markdown and scripts; the second is left to the model. Generating more Skills scales the gap, not the safety, especially when a wrong invocation can unlock a door or move money. Two adjacent attacks are documented: malicious skills compromising cloud software, and jailbroken LLM-controlled robots causing physical harm. Their intersection, malicious agent skills causing physical harm, follows directly but has not been reported. We name this class Borrowed Authority: Skills format gives the receiving agent no typed way to reject an inter-agent permission claim, so a malicious or misused Skill can drive actuation by attaching one. We propose Edge Skillguard, a typed authority layer that lives inside the Skill artifact rather than between tools as workflow engines do, with guards over world state and sensor evidence. On a live edge control-plane testbed, the guards reject 60/60 borrowed-authority requests across five attack variants without blocking benign requests, and the result holds at 5x scale and across hosts over a Tailscale mesh. These results suggest that high-risk Skills should co-package typed invocation policy with procedural knowledge, so that physical actions depend on machine-checkable evidence rather than peer-agent claims.

</details>


### [220] [Towards Reliable, Generalizable, and Specific In-Context Knowledge Editing via Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25100)
*Xuzhong Wang,Maiqi Jiang,Tejal Nair,Girija Bhusal,Yanfu Zhang,Haipeng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 MO-IKE，一种多目标强化学习算法，将上下文知识编辑中的提示构建建模为带约束的马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP），训练动态检索器以在可靠性、通用性和特异性三个竞争目标之间取得平衡，实现全局连贯的提示构建，并在 Llama-3.2 上显著提升编辑成功率、释义一致性和保留率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 的参数化知识在预训练后可能过时，知识编辑可在不重训的前提下更新模型行为。上下文知识编辑因其免训练且可应用于黑盒模型而受到关注，但现有 RL 方法虽能自适应提示构建的质量-数量权衡，却未将提示视为结构化的实体，忽略了可靠性、通用性和特异性这些不同且相互竞争的目标。以往方法大多只优化单一目标、且仅对提示构建的部分过程做决策，既未平衡各目标，也未考虑示例的全局组织。

Method: 将上下文知识编辑的提示构建形式化为约束马尔可夫决策过程（Constrained MDP），提出多目标强化学习算法 MO-IKE，训练一个动态检索器来同时优化知识编辑中的多个竞争目标，从而实现更均衡、全局一致的提示构建。

Result: 在 Llama-3.2 上，MO-IKE 将编辑成功率（可靠性）从 85.0% 提升至 92.0%，释义一致性（通用性）从 77% 提升至 79%，同时保留率（特异性）相比先前基于 RL 的方法提高了 23.0%。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模多目标权衡并以全局连贯的方式构建提示，MO-IKE 在可靠性、通用性和特异性三个维度上同时取得改进，表明将提示构建视为带约束的多目标决策问题能有效超越优化单一目标的方法。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are powerful but limited by static parametric knowledge that becomes outdated once pretraining ends. Knowledge editing addresses this problem by updating model behavior on target facts without full retraining. In particular, in-context knowledge editing has gained attention because it is training-free and readily applicable to black-box LLMs. Recent reinforcement learning (RL)-based approaches improve over fixed retrieval strategies by adapting prompt construction to the quantity-quality trade-off. Despite initial success, they fail to model the prompt as a structured entity under the distinct and often competing objectives of reliability, generality, and specificity. Previous methods largely optimize a single objective and make decisions over only part of the prompt construction process, thereby overlooking both the balance of different objectives and the global organization of demonstrations. We propose Multi-Objective In-context Knowledge Editing (MO-IKE), a multi-objective RL algorithm that formulates prompt construction for in-context knowledge editing as a Constrained Markov Decision Process. MO-IKE trains a dynamic retriever to optimize competing objectives in knowledge editing, enabling more balanced and globally coherent prompt construction. On Llama-3.2, MO-IKE improves edit success (reliability) from 85.0% to 92.0%, paraphrase consistency (generality) from 77% to 79%, while increasing retention rate (specificity) by 23.0% compared to prior RL-based methods.

</details>


### [221] [FuzzingBrain-Bench V1: Evaluating Open-Ended Bug Discovery by LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25158)
*Ze Sheng,Aleksandar Kezic,Zhicheng Chen,Jeff Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 FuzzingBrain-Bench，一个用于评估大语言模型（LLM）在开源软件中发现缺陷能力的基准测试。与现有以"命中预定义目标漏洞"为标准的基准不同，它允许模型通过消毒器插桩的 harness 触发任意不同的崩溃，并按"不同崩溃签名数量"计分。V1 版本含 77 个挑战（来自 43 个开源项目，含 36 个 C、32 个 C++ 和 9 个 Java/JVM）。在 Claude Haiku 4.5、Claude Sonnet 4.6、Claude Opus 4.8 的评测中，Opus 4.8 表现最佳，在 77 个挑战中的 60 个中触发崩溃，得分为 196/579；三个模型均无法在 13 个挑战中触发崩溃。语料与 harness 已公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有漏洞发现基准通常要求模型生成能触发"预定义目标漏洞"的 PoC 输入，但此设置可能忽略模型发现的、不符合预定义目标的合法崩溃，从而不能真实反映模型的实际能力。

Method: 构建 FuzzingBrain-Bench：将开源项目和消毒器（sanitizer）插桩的 harness 打包进自包含 Docker 镜像，让模型生成输入以尽可能触发多个不同崩溃；得分基于产生的不同崩溃签名数量，设有上限并乘以难度系数。

Result: V1 包含 77 个挑战、43 个开源项目（36 C、32 C++、9 Java/JVM）。Claude Opus 4.8 表现最佳，在 60/77 个挑战中触发崩溃，得分 196/579；Claude Haiku 4.5 与 Claude Sonnet 4.6 表现更弱；三个模型均无法在 13 个挑战中触发任何崩溃。

Conclusion: FuzzingBrain-Bench 提供了一种更贴近真实漏洞挖掘能力的评测方式，验证了不同 Claude 模型之间的能力差异，并公开了语料与 harness，以促进对 LLM 模糊测试能力的进一步研究。

Abstract: Evaluating the ability of large language models (LLMs) to discover software bugs is increasingly important. Existing benchmarks typically evaluate this capability by asking the model to generate a proof-of-concept input that triggers a predefined target vulnerability. However, this setup may overlook valid crashes discovered by the model when they do not match the predefined target. As a result, the evaluation may not reflect the model's real capability.
  We present FuzzingBrain-Bench, a benchmark for assessing AI models' ability to discover bugs in open-source software.
